                        - paragraph [ref=e317]: Make sure to lock your weapon and sceptre to both weapon sets and have your sets set correctly in both your passive tree and gems!!!

                      - heading "Strengths and Weaknesses" [level=2] [ref=e322]
                      - generic [ref=e325]:
                        - textbox [ref=e331]:
                          - list [ref=e332]:
                            - listitem [ref=e333]: Auto-Targeting Offscreen Clear
                            - listitem [ref=e334]: 30 Chains
                            - listitem [ref=e335]: No Casting Slowdown
                            - listitem [ref=e336]: 1-Button & Low Input
                        - textbox [ref=e342]:
                          - list [ref=e343]:
                            - listitem [ref=e344]:
                              - text: Needs around 5k mana and a
                              - generic [ref=e349]: Kurgal's Gaze
                              - text: before feeling tanky
                    - generic [ref=e350]:
                      - generic [ref=e351]:

                              - generic [ref=e376]: Leaguestart Absolute Minimum Entry Step 2
                            - tab "SUPER LOW BUDGET ( Warrior Variant Jewel )" [ref=e377] [cursor=pointer]:
                              - generic [ref=e378]: SUPER LOW BUDGET ( Warrior Variant Jewel )
                            - tab "Low Budget (Templar Jewel)" [ref=e379] [cursor=pointer]:

                            - tab "Medium Budget Step 2" [ref=e383] [cursor=pointer]:
                              - generic [ref=e384]: Medium Budget Step 2
                            - tab "Besos-Maxing Step 1 [ ~500-600 Div ish ]" [ref=e385] [cursor=pointer]:
                              - generic [ref=e386]: Besos-Maxing Step 1 [ ~500-600 Div ish ]
                            - tab "Besos-Maxing Step 2 [ 1100 Div ish ]" [ref=e387] [cursor=pointer]:
                              - generic [ref=e388]: Besos-Maxing Step 2 [ 1100 Div ish ]
                            - tab "Besos-Maxing Step 3 [ ~1800div ish ]" [ref=e389] [cursor=pointer]:
                              - generic [ref=e390]: Besos-Maxing Step 3 [ ~1800div ish ]
                            - tab "Elon-Maxing Step 1 [ 3300div idk ]" [ref=e391] [cursor=pointer]:
                              - generic [ref=e392]: Elon-Maxing Step 1 [ 3300div idk ]
                            - tab "The Pinnacle [ Mirror-Tier ]" [ref=e393] [cursor=pointer]:
                              - generic [ref=e394]: The Pinnacle [ Mirror-Tier ]
                            - tab "Acts Twink Spark Leveling Setup" [ref=e395] [cursor=pointer]:
                              - generic [ref=e396]: Acts Twink Spark Leveling Setup
                        - textbox [ref=e403]:
                          - paragraph [ref=e404]:
                            - text: Starter Video -
                            - link "Youtube" [ref=e405] [cursor=pointer]:
                              - /url: https://youtu.be/t6HXjq8Wnsg
                          - paragraph [ref=e406]:
                            - text: Stream -
                            - link "Twitch" [ref=e407] [cursor=pointer]:
                              - /url: https://twitch.tv/pathofky
                          - paragraph [ref=e408]:
                            - text: Unfortunately you cannot level as
                            - generic [ref=e413]: Arc
                            - generic [ref=e418]: Spell Totem
                            - text: as it requires a lot of passive points to make it to the
                            - generic [ref=e423]: Ancestral Bond
                            - text: keystone which this build requires to function.
                          - paragraph [ref=e424]: Instead we play as a Grenade Mercenary with the intent to swap to arc totems around level 65-70ish. There is unfortunately no way around this. You can play any leveling build you would like, but I have the setup I will be using laid out here.
                          - paragraph [ref=e425]:
                            - text: Here you will use
                            - generic [ref=e430]: Explosive Grenade
                            - generic [ref=e435]: Flash Grenade
                            - generic [ref=e440]: Gas Grenade
                            - generic [ref=e445]: Oil Grenade
                            - generic [ref=e450]: Cluster Grenade
                            - text: ", to speedrun through campaign."
                          - paragraph [ref=e451]:
                            - text: "Leveling POB:"
                            - link "https://pobb.in/DttTF88yOjkA" [ref=e452] [cursor=pointer]:
                              - /url: https://pobb.in/DttTF88yOjkA
                          - heading "How to Play:" [level=3] [ref=e453]
                          - heading "Act 1" [level=4] [ref=e454]
                          - paragraph [ref=e455]:
                            - text: Spend your first support gem on
                            - generic [ref=e460]: Multishot I
                            - text: for your
                            - generic [ref=e465]: Explosive Grenade
                            - text: followed by
                            - generic [ref=e470]: Multishot I
                            - text: for
                            - generic [ref=e475]: Flash Grenade
                            - text: . You should fire your
                            - generic [ref=e480]: Explosive Grenade
                            - text: far ahead of yourself and aim for the mobs to run into them as you are running away. This can take some getting used to.
                          - paragraph [ref=e481]:
                            - text: For general clear you will be using primarily
                            - generic [ref=e486]: Explosive Grenade
                            - text: ", cast it the moment you see a pack far ahead of you. This will be a long delay for now but it gets shorter and shorter as we progress."
                            - generic [ref=e491]: Flash Grenade
                            - text: can be used for a more instant but less potent burst of damage. This also causes huge stun-buildup so you should use it on cd for bosses to build stun bar.
                          - paragraph [ref=e492]:
                            - text: Try to cut a level 5 skill gem before the final boss for
                            - generic [ref=e497]: Gas Grenade
                            - text: . Your priority should loosely be
                            - generic [ref=e502]: Frost Bomb
                            - text: ( for exposure as needed ) >
                            - generic [ref=e507]: Explosive Grenade
                            - text: ">"
                            - generic [ref=e512]: Gas Grenade
                            - text: ">"
                            - generic [ref=e517]: Flash Grenade
                            - text: ( for stun buildup. ) As long as the mob is ingited
                            - generic [ref=e522]: Gas Grenade
                            - text: will pop instantly.
                          - paragraph [ref=e523]:
                            - strong [ref=e524]: "Focus on +# to # added X damage on your crossbow and both rings. It is INCREDIBLY important to keep this value as high as possible as it is the baseline for all your skills damage."
                            - text: Check Vendors constantly to keep your weapon and rings up to date.
                          - paragraph [ref=e525]:
                            - text: "For Armour Pieces look to use Either Armour or Armour/Evasion Hyrbid Pieces with:"
                            - text: . +# to Maximum Life
                            - text: . +#% Increased Cold/Fire/Lightning Resistnace
                            - text: . High Total Aarmour/Evasion Rating Value

                    - generic [ref=e1237]:
                      - heading "How it Plays" [level=2] [ref=e1241]
                      - textbox [ref=e1248]:
                        - heading "FAQ" [level=3] [ref=e1249]
                        - paragraph [ref=e1250]:
                          - text: Its VERY important for single target that you have your weapon set passives set correctly in both your passive tree and gem assignments.
                          - strong [ref=e1251]: Also MAKE SURE your last cast is either a totem or mana tempest so that you are resting in set 1 ( archmage ).
                        - heading "When do I swap?" [level=4] [ref=e1252]
                        - paragraph [ref=e1253]: Swap when you have a gear set prepared to put you around 3.5k mana and you are AT MINIMUM level 70. BOTH OF THESE TOGETHER ARE REQUIRED not 1 or the other. Mana regen on rings and spirit on both your chest and amulet are very important aswell.
                        - heading "Upgrade order?" [level=4] [ref=e1254]
                        - paragraph [ref=e1255]: Buy Low Basic Stater Set of Gears -> !gring, !wand -> Buy/Semi-Craft a Cheap Medium Budget Amulet / Scepter / Helm / Chest / Boots / Gloves !gamulet -> !gbody -> !ghelm -> !ggloves -> !fork -> Mageblood -> !ggbody -> !gsceptre
